68b2(phal chen): ltar rgya mtsho'i chu chen dag/tshad med rnyog pa med pa ltar//sangs rgyas mthong ba'ang de bzhin te//'jig rten kun gyi skom po gcod//rgya mtsho chu las 'phags pa yi//ri bo ri rab ci 'dra ba//de bzhin 'jig 
68b3(phal chen): rten sgron ma yang //chos kyi mtsho las mngon par 'phags////rin chen kun gyi 'byung gnas kyis//rgya mtsho rgyas pa ji bzhin du//rang byung ye shes de bzhin te//mi zad skad cig mkhyen pa//mgon 
68b4(phal chen): po'i ye shes shin tu zab//tshad med de bzhin bgrang du med//de bas sangs rgyas rnam 'phrul pa//bsam gyis mi khyab tshad med ston//sgyu ma'i mtshan nyid ston pa yi//sgyu mkhan mkhas pa ji lta ba//
68b5(phal chen): da ltar sangs rgyas mkhyen dbang yang //rnam par 'phrul pa rab tu ston//yid bzhin nor bu rnam dag pa//bsam don rdzogs byed ji lta ba//de bzhin bsam pa dag pa la//rgyal ba smon lam rdzogs pa 
68b6(phal chen): mdzad//'od snang gsal ba byed pa yi//rin chen rnam snang ji lta ba//de ltar thams cad mkhyen dag pa//'gro ba'i bsam pa gsal bar mdzad//ji ltar phyogs la mngon blta'o//rin chen zur 
68b7(phal chen): brgyad rab gnas pa//de bzhin chags med sgron ma yang //chos dbyings rab tu snang ba mdzad//chu dang rin chen 'od gsal ba//ji ltar dra med chu 'dang byed//de bzhin sangs rgyas mthong bas kyang //
68b8(phal chen): 'gro ba'i dbang po dag par 'gyur//de nas byang chub sems dpa' chos kyi dbyings su smon lam rab tu 'phrul ba'i zla ba'i rgyal pos//sangs rgyas kyis byin gyis rlabs kyi phyogs bcur rnam par 
69a1(phal chen): @#//bltas te/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//phyogs 'di yin dra nI la yi//kha dog mdog cig byas pa ltar//sangs rgyas mthong bas skye dgu rnams//
69a2(phal chen): de bzhin byang chub mdog tu 'gyur//sangs rgyas rdul phran re re la//rnam par 'phrul pa sna tshogs rnams//tshad med gzhal du med par ston//byang chub sems dpa' rnam dag mdzad//de ni rmad 
69a3(phal chen): byung shin tu zab//mtha' yas bsgrub par dka' ba ste//blo ldan ye shes yul de la//'jig rten nams ni 'bab mi nus//byang chub sems dpa' bsgrubs don du//bkod pa phun sum tshogs 
69a4(phal chen): pa dang //sangs rgyas tshul gyis rnam sbyangs pa'i//chos kyi dbyings la 'jug pa ston//sangs rgyas zhing rnams mtha' yas pa//blo ldan bskor ba'i sangs rgyas kyis//kun nas gang 
69a5(phal chen): bar gyur pa yang //rgyal ba de la ston pa mdzad//chos kun mnga' sgyur ston pa ste//shAkya'i khyu mchog skyes par 'gyur//de yi cho 'phrul tshad med pas//'di dag rtag tu byung ba yin//
69a6(phal chen): brtan pa'i spyod pa sna tshogs rnams//tshad? med rnam par gzigs kun nas//rnam par 'phrul ba mtha' yas pa//tshad med gzi can ston pa mdzad//'jig rten mgon po rgyal sras rnams//
69a7(phal chen): chos kyi dbyings la slob pa mdzad//chos rnams thams cad yongs la yang //chags med ye shes spyod yul gyur//skyes bu dbang po'i byin rlabs kyis//cho 'phrul brgya dang rab ldan 
69a8(phal chen): zhing //'jig rten thams cad rnam spyod pa'i//chos kyi 'khor lo rab tu bskor//sems can gtso bo'i yul la yang //ye shes dkyil 'khor yongs sbyongs shing //shes rab 
69b1(phal chen): chen po'i klu chen rnams//'jig rten thams cad 'grel par 'gyur//de nas byang chub sems dpa' chos kyi don gyi blo gros gzi brjid rgyal pos//bcom ldan 'das kyi byin gyi rlabs kyis//
69b2(phal chen): phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa'i dag smras so//drang srong dam pa'i mchog rnams kyi//dus gsum nyan thos gang byung ba//de dag kun gyis bde gshegs kyis//
69b3(phal chen): gshegs thabs 'jog 'deg mi shes su//dus gsum ma lus thams cad kyi//ma nor rang rgyal gang byung ba//de dag gis kyang mgon po yi//gshegs thabs 'jog 'di ga mi rig go//ma rig mun 
69b4(phal chen): pas bsgribs pa rnams//khyi ltar ltung la rgyus pa yi//sems can de 'dra kun gyis ni//mgon po mi shes smos mi dgos//rgyal ba tshad mas gzhal ba min//de ni shes par mi nus te//sangs 
69b5(phal chen): rgyas chags med ye shes can//tshig gi lam las shin tu 'das//zla ba nya rgyas 'od mnga' ba//brtan pa mtshan gyis rnam par bris//de ni bskal pa dpag yas par//rnam 'phrul thogs 
69b6(phal chen): med spyod pa mdzad//tshul sgo re res sangs rgyas la//shin tu mnyam bzhag bsam byas kyang //bskal pa bye ba bsam yas pa//brjod du med bzang gyur gyi//rang byung rnams kyi yon tan ni//
69b7(phal chen): phyogs gcig cha yis mthar mi phyin//sangs rgyas de yi zhal bltas kyang //sangs rgyas chos ni bsam mi khyab//gang zhig de la smon byed cing //gang zhig yid kyis dga' mos pa//de dag kun gyi de 
69b8(phal chen): 'dra ba'i//spyod yul blta dka' 'thob par 'gyur//bsod nams ye shes mtha' yas dang //tshogs can yangs pas rnam gnon pa//dri mar blo ldan sems gnas pa//de dag 'di yi tshul la 
70a1(phal chen): @#//'jug/de dag smon lam rgya che bas//sems dang sdom pa rgya che ste//rgyal ba'i spyod yul thob gyur cig/byang chub rgya che na gnon par 'gyur//de nas byang chub sems 
70a2(phal chen): dpa' bdud rtsi dkyil 'khor rnam par 'thor ba'i ye shes rgyal mtshan gyi rgyal pos//sangs rgyas kyi byin gyis rlabs kyis phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa'i de dag smras so//
70a3(phal chen): chags med ye shes sku mnga' bas/rang byung rnams ni sku med de//bsam yas ye shes sku mnga' bas//de dag bsam par mi nus so//phrin las dkar po bsam yas pas//sangs rgyas sku rab 
70a4(phal chen): bsgrub pa'i phyir//de la 'jig rten gsum gyi mgon//mtshan dang dpe byad bzang pos mdzes//chos kyi dbyings kyis rnam sbyangs pas//'jig rten kun tu snang ba mdzad//sangs rgyas byang chub sgo yin te//
70a5(phal chen): ye shes kun gyis gnas chen no//chags pa thams cad rnam srangs pas//rdul med spros pa mi mnga'o//ye shes 'od zer rab tu byed pas//'jig rten kun du nyi ma 'dra//khams gsum thams cad rnam 
70a6(phal chen): sbyongs pas//srid pa'i 'jigs pa rnam par gcod//byang chub sems dpa'i bsgrub pa mdzad//byang chub 'byung gnas de bzhin no//kha dog mtha' las ston mdzad kyang //kha dog kun la gnas 
70a7(phal chen): pa med//sems can kun gyis bsam yas pa'i//kha dog tshad med yangs su ston//skad cig gcig la sangs rgyas kyi//byang chub bsam yas sbyangs pas na//de yis ye shes mthar byin 
70a8(phal chen): par//rtag par sus kyang mi nus so//ye shes bstan pa zad mi shes//rang bzhin rnam par 'gyur ba med//de la dus gsum rgyal ba kun//skad cig gcig gis rab tu brjod//rtag pa las gzhan yongs 
70b1(phal chen): btang ste//shes ldan byang chub don gnyer bas//sems la sems shes bya ba yang //de la sems skye med par bsam//gang gi sangs rgyas bskyed pa yi//sangs rgyas chos rnams bsam mi khyab//
70b2(phal chen): de dag brjod pa'i yul ma yin//shin tu zab pas tshig lam spangs//de nas byang chub sems dpa' rnam par snang mdzad kyi smon lam dpal gyi rgyas mtshan gyis/sangs rgyas kyi byin gyis rlabs kyi phyogs 
70b3(phal chen): bcur rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//dran pa rmongs med dag pa rnams//chos 'dzin shin tu des rtogs shing //blo gros bsam gyis mi khyab pa//byang chub rgya mtsho 
70b4(phal chen): mi zad pa//de dag yid ni 'di dang nges//de dag spyod pa'i spyod yul yin//de dag shes pa 'dir mi g.yo//de dag 'di la the tsom chad//de dag skyo bar mi 'gyur te//de dag yid kyang zhum ma 'gyur//sangs rgyas chos 
70b5(phal chen): la rab gzhol bar//de dag sems kyang rab tu 'byung //de dag dang ba ting tshugs shing //yang sngags phags pa rab tu bskyed//dmigs med gnas pa med pa yi//ye shes 'dis de dag dga'//bskal 
70b6(phal chen): pa bye bar bsags pa yi//yongs su rdzogs pa'i dge ba'i chos//ye shes 'di 'dod mtshungs pa med//de dag thams cad yongs su bsngo//'khor ba'i nang na rgyu ba yang //'khor ba dag la gnas 
70b7(phal chen): pa min//sangs rgyas chos rnams rab rtogs pas//sangs rgyas yul la rab tu brtse//sems can khams ni ji snyed du//'jig rten phun sum tshogs rgyu ba//de dag thams cad brtan pas 
70b8(phal chen): spangs//sangs rgyas phun sum tshogs la gnas////sangs rgyas rtag tu 'byung gyur kyang //'jig rten don med rab tu gnas//chags med spyod pa de dag ni//rtag tu sems can don la gnas//

71b1(phal chen): gang gis rgyal ba bsnyen bkur ba//de dag ngan song 'jigs pa med//rkang gnyis dam pa mthong gyur na//shin tu yangs pa'i sems skye ste//'di snang gsal ba'i shes rab mthu//grangs med de bzhin skye bar 
71b2(phal chen): 'gyur//sangs rgyas skyes bu'i mchog mthong zhing //byang chub dam par nges par 'gyur//de bzhin gshegs par 'gyur bya zhes//de dag kun kyang nges par sems//de nas byang chub sems dpa' chos kyi dbyings

71b7(phal chen): sangs rgyas mi mthong ma yin no//'jig rten 'di dag thams cad na//tha dad ngan song rgyud yod pa//de dag yun ring gnas bla yi//sangs rgyas mi mthong ma yin//sems can dmyal ba re re yang //
71b8(phal chen): bskal pa tshad med gnas bla yi//mtha' yas rgyal bas brnyes pa yis//byang chub mchog las ring gnas min//ci zhig phyir zhing ngan yod du//yun ring gnas par 'dod chen na//rgyal ba'i 
72a1(phal chen): @#//dbang po mthong ba ni/ye shes 'phel ba'i rgyur 'gyur phyir//rgyal ba 'jig rten mchog mthong na//sdug bsngal thams cad 'chad par 'gyur//rdzogs pa'i 
72a2(phal chen): sangs rgyas spyod yul gyi//ye shes khong du chud par 'byung //sangs rgyas myi yi mchog mthong zhing //sgrib pa thams cad zad par 'gyur//byang chub gang gis 'thob pa yi//bsod nams tshad med 'phel 
72a3(phal chen): bar 'gyur//sangs rgyas mthong bar sems can rnams//the tsom yid gnyis chad par 'gyur//'jig rten 'das dang 'jig rten gyi//bsam pa'ang yongs su rdzogs par 'gyur//de nas byang chub sems

72a5(phal chen): dang rgya cher brjod pa dang /gsal bar bya ba dang rab tu brjod pa dang /yang dag par bstan pa dang /shin tu snang bar bya ba dang /rab tu gsal bar bya ba dang man ngag bstan pa'i phyir/chos kyi dbyings kyi 
72a6(phal chen): tshul nam mkha'i dbyings dang mnyam pas/dus gsum mnyam pa nyid dang /chos kyi dbyings mnyam pa nyid dang sems can gyi khams mnyam pa nyid dang /'jig rten thams cad mnyam pa nyid dang las kyi rgyud 
72a7(phal chen): thams cad mnyam pa nyid dad sems can gyi bsam pa mnyam pa nyid dang /sems can gyi mos pa mnyam pa nyid dad chos kyi snang ba mnyam pa nyid dang /sem can yongs su smin par bya ba'i dus mnyam pa 
72a8(phal chen): nyid dang /'gro ba thams cad kyi dbang po mnyam pa nyid kyis/de bzhin gshegs pa'i ting nge 'dzin seng ge rnam par bsgyings pa/'di nyid byang chub sems dpa' de dag la bstan pa'i tshul bcus yang dag 
72b1(phal chen): par rab tu ston te/bcu gang zhe na/'di ltar chos ltar chos kyi dbyings thams cad du gtogs pa'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi rdul shin tu phra ba rnams la/sangs rgyas gcig nas gcig tu rgyud pa bstan pa dang /
72b2(phal chen): 'di lta ste/nam mkha'i dbyings kyis klas pa'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams cad du/phyi ma'i mtha'i mur thug pa'i skal bar de bzhin gshegs pa'i yon tan rjes su brjod pa bstan pa dang /'di lta ste/sangs 
72b3(phal chen): rgyas kyi zhing thams cad du de bzhin gshegs pa yongs su skye ba dang /mtha' dang dbus med pa'i mngon par rdzogs pa'i byang chub kyi sgo rgya mtsho yongs su brjod cing bstan pa dang /'di ltar ste nam mkha'i dbyings 
72b4(phal chen): kyi klas pa'i sangs rgyas kyi zhi gi de bzhin gshegs pa'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor gyi byang chub sems dpa'i dge 'dun snying po'i byang chub la mngon du gyur pa'i gnas dang /'di lta ste dus gsum gyi sangs rgyas kyi 
72b5(phal chen): sku dang 'od zer sprul pa ba spu'i sgo thams cad nas/sems skad cig la rab tu bkye zhing chos kyi dbyings rgyas par dgang ba bstan pa dang /'di lta sto phyogs rgya mtsho thams cad du sangs rgyas kyi zhing rgya mtsho gzhi mnyam 
72b6(phal chen): par byin gyis rlabs cing lus gcig gi rgyas par dgang ba bstan ba dang /'di lta ste dmigs pa'i gzhi thams cad la sangs rgyas kyi rnam par sprul ba dus gsum thams cad yang dag par gzhol bar 'gyur ba'i

72b8(phal chen): pa rgya mtsho yongs su bshad cing bstan pa dang /'di ltar ste dus gsum gyi sangs rgya kyi smon lam rgya mtsho thams cad ba spu'i sgo thams cad ni sa rab tu brjod pa/phyi ma'i mtha' byin gyis rlabs pas 
73a1(phal chen): @#//byang chub sems dpa' yang dag par 'byung ba bstan pa dang /'di lta ste chos kyi dbyings dang mnyam pa'i seng ge'i khri dang /byang chub sems dpa'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor 
73a2(phal chen): dang /snying po byang chub kyi rgyan tha mi dad pa dang /chos kyi 'khor lo rnam pa tha mi dad par rab tu bskor ba/phyi ma'i mthar byin gyis rlabs pa bstan pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras de ltar seng ge rnam par 
73a3(phal chen): bsgyings pa/ting nge 'dzin 'di la bstan pa bcu po de dag la sogs pa shin tu bstan pa sangs rgyas kyi zhing brjod du med pa'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed yod de/kye rgyal ba'i sras dag de ni/de bzhin gshegs 
73a4(phal chen): pa'i ye shes kyi spyod yul lo//de nas byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang pos de bzhin gshegs pa'i ting nge 'dzin chen po seng+ge rnam par bsgyings pa'i 'di nyid kyi don gyi phyogs bstan pa'i phyir 
73a5(phal chen): sangs rgyas kyi byin gyis rlabs kyi de bzhin gshegs pa'i zhal tu lta zhing 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor rgya mtsho thams cad la rnam par bltas nas/sangs rgyas kyi yul bsam gyis mi khyab pa dang /
73a6(phal chen): de bzhin gshegs pa'i ting de 'dzin gyi rnam par 'phrul pa mtha' dang dbus med pa la rnam par bltas te/'jig rten gyi khams bsam gyis mi khyab par gzhal ba la rnam par ltas nas ye shes

73b1(phal chen): dang mnyam/de nas sangs rgyas byang chub sems dpa'i 'khor//kun gyis yongs bskar sangs rgyas gdan la bzhugs//spu gcig rtse la zhing mang rgya mtsho rnams//snying po byang chub padamo'i gdan 
73b2(phal chen): gnas pas//chos dbyings rgya chen yongs su rgyas bkang zhing //shing drung dam pa kun na mgon po snang //zhing rnams kun gyi rdul phran rab mang rnyed//rgyal ba spu rtse gcig la yongs zhugs pa//byang chub

74b1(phal chen): dpa'i tshogs kyis kun nas bskor ba/bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu mngon par 'tshang rgya bar mthong ste/la la ni sangs rgyas kyi zhing brjod du med pa tsam du shin tu yangs pa'i 'khor 
74b2(phal chen): kyi dkyil 'khor na/chos kyi dbyings rgyas par khyab pa'i dbyangs kyi dkyil 'khor gyis chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba mthong ngo//la la ni lha'i gnas na 'dug/la la na ni klu'i gnas na 'dug/
74b3(phal chen): la la na ni gnad sbyin gyi gnas na 'dug/la la ni ni lha ma yin gyi gnas na 'dug/la na ni nam mkha' lding gi gnas na 'dug/la la nani mi 'am ci'i gnas na 'dug/la lan ni lho 'phye 
74b4(phal chen): chen po'i gnas na 'dug/la la la ni mi'i dbang po'i nang na 'dug/la la na ni mi'i 'jig rten gyi grong dang /grong khyer dang /grong brdal dang ljongs dang /khams dang /rgyal po'i pho brang 
74b5(phal chen): 'khor dag na rnam par 'phrul pa sna tshogs dang /spyod lam sna tshogs dang /lus sna tshogs dang /ting nge 'dzin gyi sgo rnam par dmigs pa sna tshogs dang /ting nge mdzin gyi mngon par shes 
74b6(phal chen): pa sna tshogs dang /rigs dang rgyud sna tshogs su byung ba dang /kha dog rnam par dmigs pa sna tsho gas dang /'od kyi dkyil 'khor sna tshogs dang /'od zer gyi dra ba bkye ba sna tshogs dang /
74b7(phal chen): dbyangs kyi dkyil 'khor sna tshogs pa dang /gtam gyi skyes bu'i gzhi sna tshogs dang /bstan pa'i gzhi sna tshogs dang /tshig dang yi ge dang nges pa'i tshig sna tshogs kyis 'khor gyi dkyil 
74b8(phal chen): 'khor sna tshogs na chos ston pa mthong bar gyur to//byang chub sems dpa' ji snyed kyi 'jig rten gyi khams chos kyi dbyings kyis klas pa/nam mkha'i mthas gtugs pa phyogs bcu 
75a1(phal chen): @#//na rnam par gnas pa/phyogs kyi rgyud mtha' yas par 'dus pa/phyogs rgya mtsho thams cad du gtogs pa/chos sna tshogs kyi sgo dang ldan pa/
75a2(phal chen): phyogs kyi ming du bya ba sna tshogs su gtogs pa/phyogs sna tshogs kun tu gzhol ba/phyogs cha rnam pa tha dad par gtogs pa/phyogs sna tshog kyi rjes su song ba/phyogs 
75a3(phal chen): rgya mtsho sna tshogs su gtogs ba 'di lta ste/shar phyogs dang /lho phyogs dang /nub phyags dang /byang phyogs dang /byang dang shar gyi phyogs mtshams dang /shar dang lho'i 
75a4(phal chen): phyogs mtshams dang /lho dang nub kyi phyogs mtshams dang /nub dang byang gi phyogs mtshams dang /'og dang steng gi phyogs kyi zhing gi lhun gyi phyogs dang /sems can gyi lus 
75a5(phal chen): kyi phyogs dang /sems canagyi ming du bya ba'i phyogs dang /sngon gyi mtha'i mur thugs pa'i phyogs dang /phyogs bcu'i da ltar byung ba'i phyogs dang /nam mkha'i khongs thams cad 
75a6(phal chen): du sgra'i rtse mo shin tu phra ba dgod pas dmigs pa'i phyogs dang /zhing thams cad kyi rdul shin tu phra ba gcig nas gcig tu brgyud pa'i phyogs la 'jug pa khong du chud par bya ba'i phyogs dang /las 
75a7(phal chen): kyi 'du byed sna tshogs kyis yongs su grub pa'i phyogs dang /skra'i rtse mo gcig la mtha' dang dbus med pa'i nam mkha'i dbyings kyi ming du bya bar gtogs pa'i phyogs kyi khongs su chud pa'i dus 
75a8(phal chen): gsum gyi dbyings mnyam pa nyid du gzhol bas/kun tu gtogs pa'i 'gro ba thams cad tha mi dad pas/sems can thams cad kyi 'du shes kyi rnam pa la mnyam pa'i sgra dbyangs su 
75b1(phal chen): gyur ba'i 'gro ba thams cad kyi sems la gzugs brnyan rab tu snang bar gyur pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa'i sangs rgyas kyi ting nge 'dzin rnam par 'grub pa/sems can thams cad kyi lus la mngon 
75b2(phal chen): par lta ba/dmigs par bya bas 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor thams cad du 'gro ba'i tshal dang ldan pa/bskal pa thams cad la ye shes pa mi dang bzhir thams cad la kun tu snyoms pas sems can 
75b3(phal chen): gyi bsam pa ji lta ba de bzhin du gzugs mngon sum du bstan pa rnam par dmigs pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa'i rnam par 'phrul pa sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad yang dag par stan pas/sems can 
75b4(phal chen): thams cad 'dul ba rgyun mi 'chad pa mthong bar gyur pa de dag thams cad ni sngon/bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyis dge ba'i spyod pa bskal pa mnyam pa dang /bsdu ba'i dngos po bzhis 
75b5(phal chen): yongs su bsdus pa mthong ba dang mnyam pa dang /rjes su dran pa bsnyen bkur bas yongs su smin par byas pa dag go/sngon bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu sems bskyed pa la btsud 
75b6(phal chen): pa dag go/de bzhin gshegs pa de dang de dag gi drung du gshegs pa'i tshe dge ba'i rtsa bas longs su bsdus pa dag ste/de ltar dge ba'i rtsa ba bskal pa mnyam pas/thams cad 
75b7(phal chen): mkhyen pa nyid du yongs su smin par 'gyur ba'i thabs kyis shin tu bzung ba'i phyir/bcom ldan 'das rnam bar snang mdzad kyi ting nge 'dzin gyi rnam par 'phrul pa chos kyi dbyings ltar yangs shing 
75b8(phal chen): nam mkha'i dbyings kyi mthas gtugs par gyur pa dag kyang mtha' zhing khong du chud par gyur pa yin te/la la ni chos kyi sku la 'jug go/la la ni gzugs kyi sku la 'jug go/lal ni sngon 
76a1(phal chen): @#//gyi byang chub sems dpa'i yongs su bsgrub pa la 'jug go/la la ni pha rol tu phyin pa yongs su rdzogs pa la 'jug go/la la ni/spyod pa'i dkyil 'khor 
76a2(phal chen): yongs su dag pa'i rgyan la 'jug go/la la ni byang chub sems dpa'i sa yi rnam par 'phrul pa la 'jug go/la la nimangon par byang chub pa'i rnam par 'phrul pa la 'jog go/la la ni 
76a3(phal chen): sangs rgyas rnam par spyod pa'i ting nge 'dzin tha mi dad pa'i rnam par 'phrul pa 'jug go/la la ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs dang mi bsnyengs pa la 'jug go/la lani sangs rgyas kyi so so

76a5(phal chen): tshogs dang lam sna tshogs dang /sgo sna tshogs dang 'jug pa sna tshogs dang /'bab pa sna tshogs dang rnam pa sna tshogs dang /rjes su 'brang ba sna tshogs dang phyogs sna tshogs dang snod sna tshogs dang 
76a6(phal chen): yul sna tshogs dang 'jig rten sna tshogs dang khong du chud pa sna tshogs dang /tshogs sna tshogs dang rnam 'phrul sna tshogs dang /thabs sna tshogs dang ting nge 'dzin sna tshogs kyis sangs rgyas kyi rnam par 
76a7(phal chen): brgya mtsho de dag la 'jug go/ting nge 'dzin sna tshogs kyi 'jug pa 'di lta ste/byang chub sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin chos kyi dbyings kun nas brgyan pa dang /byang chub sems dpa'i teng nge 'dzin 
76a8(phal chen): dus gsum thams cad la thogs pa med par yul snang bar byed pa dang /byang chub semasad pa'i ting nge 'dzin chos kyis dbyings kyi gzhi tha mi dad pa'i ye shes snang ba dang /byang chub sems pa'i 
76b1(phal chen): ting nge 'dzin de bzhin gshegs pa'i yul gyi dbyings la 'jug pa dang /byang chub sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin nam mkha'i dbyings su snang ba dang /byang chub sems pa'i ting nge 'dzin de bzhin gsheDa 
76b2(phal chen): pa'i stobs bcu 'thob pas rnam par 'byed pa dang /byang chub semasadapa'i ting nge 'dzin sangs rgyas kyi snyengs pa mi mnga' ba'i rgyan gyis rnam par gnon cing rnam par bsgyings pa dang /byang chub sem
76b3(phal chen): sa dpa'i ting nge 'dzin chos kyi dbyings kyi tshul thams cad kyi klong gi snying po dang /byang chub sems dba'i ting nge 'dzin chos kyi rabyings thams cad du thogs pa med par sgra sgrog pas rgyas par 'gongs 
76b4(phal chen): pa'i zla ba dang /byang chub sem dpa'i ting nge 'dzin ches kyi dbyings mnyam pa nyid kyi brgyan pa'i 'od kyi byang chub sems dpa' de dag bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyi sangs rgyas kyi rnam par

76b7(phal chen): pa'i sku tha dad pa'i yul la 'jug pa dang /byang chub sems ba'i ting nge 'dzin 'jig rten thams cad kyi rgyud kyi rjes su 'jug pa snying rje chen po'i snying po can dang /byang chub semasadapa'i ting nge 'dzin chos 
76b8(phal chen): thams cad kyi gnas la brten pa'i byin gyis rlabs dang /byang chub sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzan shin tu zhi la rab tu zhi bas kun tu snang ba'i dkyil 'khor dang /byang chub sems nga'i ting nge 'dzin sprul pa 
77a1(phal chen): @#//bzang po dmigs su med pas/kun tu 'phrul pa'i gzugs brnyan dang /byang chub sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin zhing thams cad yongs su 'du ba'i byin gyis rlabs dang /

77a4(phal chen): bskyed pa las byung ba'i byin gyis rlabs dang byang chub sems dpa'i ting 'dzin yon tan rgya mtsho thams cad la nan tan gyi 'jug pa dang /byang chub semasad pa'i ting nge 'dzin dmigs pa ma lus pa

77b4(phal chen): yongs su ston pa'i byin gyis rlabs dang /byang chub sems pa'i ting nge 'dzin spyi'u tshugs su gzhi la rnam par spyod pa'i byin gyis rlabs pa dang /byang chub se mas dpa'i ting nge 'dzin na sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams 
77b5(phal chen): cad kyi sems can gyi lus tha mi dad par byin gyis rlobs pa dang /byang chub kyi sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin ye shes kyi klong thams cad la mngon sum du yang dag par gzhol ba dang /byang chub sems dpa'i ting

78a3(phal chen): dang /byang chub sems dba'i ting nge 'dzin chos gcig la chos thams cad yongs su 'du bar ston pa dang /byang chub semsdpa'i ting nge 'dzin chos gcigagi tshig gi lam la nges pa'i ngag gi rab tu 'byed pas/

79a1(phal chen): @#//sgrogs pa dang /byang chub sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin sangs rgyas thams cad mthong ba'i sa mtshams 'dzin pa dang /byang chub semasad pa'i ting nge 'dzin chos kyi dbyings

103a5(phal chen): mtho ris dang mya ngan las 'das pa'i sgo 'byed pa dang /khams gsum gyi grong khyer gyi sgo rnam par gcod cing thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi pho brang gi sgo rnam par 'byed pa dang /yo byad thams

103a7(phal chen): nor bzangs la 'di skad ces smras so//rigs kyi bu khyod kyis de ltar bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu sems bskyed pa legs so legs so//rigs kyi bu dge ba'i 
103a8(phal chen): rtsa ba ma bskyed pa'i sems can rnams ni byang chub tu sems bskyedas kyang mi skye ba'i dge ba'i rtsa ba sgo kun tu snang ba rab tu thob pa dang /thabs kyi snying po dang ldan pa'i lam gyi ting nge 'dzin gyi ye 
103b1(phal chen): shes snang bas rab tu snang bar gyur ba dang /bsod nams kyi rgya mtsho'i tshogs rgya chen po bsags pa dang /dkar po'i tshogs thams cad kyi rgyun mi 'chad pa dang /dge ba'i bshes 
103b2(phal chen): gnyen thams cad bkur bas yongs su skyo ba med pa dang /lus dang srog la 'phangs pa mi brtsi ba dang /dngos por 'dzin pa thams cad dang bral ba dang /mtho dman med pa'i sa ltar sems 
103b3(phal chen): mnyam pa dang /rang bzhin gyi snying rje che zhing byams par sems pa dang ldan pa dang /srid pa'i lam gyi rgyud thams cad du gnas par 'jug pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa'i yul la mngon 
103b4(phal chen): par mos pa'i sems can rnams la byang chub kyi sems skye ste/'di lta bu sems can thams cad yongs su bskyob pa'i phyir snying rje chen po'i sems dang 'gro ba thams cad la snyoms par nan 
103b5(phal chen): tan du bya ba'i phyir byams pa'i sems dang /'gro ba thams cad kyi sdug bsngal gyi phung po zhi bar bya ba'i phyir bde ba'i sems dang /mi dge ba'i chos thams cad las rnam par bzlog pa'i phyir 
103b6(phal chen): phan pa'i sems dang /'jigs pa thams cad las yongs su bsrungs pa'i phyir snying brtse ba'i sems dang /sgrib pa thams cad rnam par bzlog pa'i phyir/chags pa med pa'i sems dang /

103b8(phal chen): gshegs pa thams cad mthong zhing rnam par dmigs par bya ba'i phyir dri ma med pa'i sems dang /dus gsum gyi dmigs pa ma lus par ye shes kyis rgyas par dgad pa'i phyir rnam par dag pa'i 
104a1(phal chen): @#//sems dang /thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes rgya mtsho la 'jug par bya ba'i phyir ye shes kyi sems kyang skye bar 'gyur ro//rigs kyi bu kho bos ni phyogs 
104a2(phal chen): kyi ljongs rgya mtsho'i sgo 'dir lo bcu gnyis su rgya mtsho chen po 'di la dmigs te/mngon du byas nas 'di ltar rgya mtsho rgya che zhing tshad med pa la rjes su bsam pa dang /dri ma med cing dad pa 
104a3(phal chen): dang /zab cing gting dpag dka' ba dang /bags kyi gzhol zhing legs par gnas pa dang /rin po che'i 'byung gnas du mas rnam par brgyan pa dang /chu'i phung po tshad med pa dang /

104b1(phal chen): rin po che a sma gar ba'i ze ba can/rgya mtsho tsam du yangs shing rgya che ba lha ma yin gyi dbang po brgya stong phrag bcus sdong po'i lhan nas bzung ba/rin po che sna tshogs brgya phrag stong gi dra bas 
104b2(phal chen): kun nas g.yog pa/klu yi dbang po brgya stong phrag bcas spos kyi chu'i char mngon par phab pa/nam mkha' lding gi dbang po brgya stong phrag bcos dar dang rin po che'i lda ldi dang se mdo mang po bcus 
104b3(phal chen): bzung zhing rab tu sbyangs pa mi 'am ci'i dbang po brgya stong phrag bdus phan pa'i sems kyis kun nas blta ba/lto 'phye chen po'i dbang po brgya stong phrag pa bcus 'khor nas mngon par 'dud pa/srin po'i 
104b4(phal chen): dbang po brgya stong phrag bcu sa 'dud cing mngon par mchod pa/dri za'i dbang po brgya stong phrag cus rol mo dang glu sna tshogs snyan pas bstad cing mchod pa/lha'i dbang po brgya stong phrag gcus lha 
104b5(phal chen): rdzas kyi me tog dang /spos dang phreng ba dang /bdug spos dang /byug pa dang /phye ma dang /gos dang /gdugs dang /rgyal mtshan dang /lha'i dbang pos sprin gyi char mngon par phab pa/tshangs 
104b6(phal chen): pa'i dbang po brgya stong phrag dbus spyi bo nas gtugs te/phyag 'tshal zhing mchod pa/gnas gtsang ma'i ris kyi lha brgya stong phrag bcus thal mo sbyar cing phyag bya ba/mi'i dbang po 'khor 
104b7(phal chen): lo bsgyur ba brgya stong phrag bcus rin po che bdun gyis bsu zhing mngon par mchod pa/rgya mtsho'i lha brgya stong phrag bcus mngon par mthon cing phyag bya ba/nor bu rin po che skar ma'i ngang tshul 
104b8(phal chen): brgya stong phrag bcus 'od zer gyi rgyan gyis rab tu snang bar byas pa/nor bu rin po che bsod nams kyis dag pa brgya stong phrag bcus mkhos su phebs par 'brel bas rab tu brgyan pa/nor bu 
105a1(phal chen): @#//rin po che rnam par snang ba brgya stong phrag bcus snying po rab tu gsal bar byas pa/nor bu rin po che dpal gyi snying po brgya stong phrag bcus dpal chen po shin tu 'bar 
105a2(phal chen): bar byas pa/nor bu rin bo che'i mdzod snang ba brgya stong phrag bcas mtha' yas par snang bar byas nas/nor bu rin po che 'dzam bu'i chu klung brgya stong phrag bcus yongs su ston par 
105a3(phal chen): gnas pas rab tu brgyan pa/nor bu rin po che rdo rje seng ge brgya stong phrag bcus thub pa med par brgyan pa/nor bu rin bo de nyi ma'i snying po brgya stong phrag bcas rgya cher gsal bar bsgrub 
105a4(phal chen): pa/nor bu rin po che 'bar ba brgya stong phrag bcus kha dog sna tshogs su brgyan pa/nor bu rin pa che yid bzhin gyi rgyal po brgya stong phrag bcus mi zad pa'i brgyan pa 'od 'bar bar byas pa byung 
105a5(phal chen): ste/pad+ma chen po da ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i 'jig rten las 'das pa'i dge ba'i rtsa ba las byung ba/byang chub sems dpa'i bsam pas yongs su brten pa'o//phyogs thams cad du 
105a6(phal chen): mngon par dmigs su yod pa'o//sgyu ma'i rnam pa chos kyi byung ba'o//rjen pa'i dri ma med pa las byung ba'o//nyon mongs pa med pa'i chos kyi tshul gyi brgyan pa'o//rmi lam lta bu'i chos 
105a7(phal chen): nyid kyis yongs su byung ba'o//mngon par 'du bya ba med pa'i tshul gyis rgyas btab pa'o//chags pa med pa'i chos kyi tshuladang ldan pa'o//phyogs kyi khams kun tu chos kyi dbyings 
105a8(phal chen): rgyas par 'gengs pa'o//sangs rgyas kyi yul brjod pa dang 'thun pa ste/de'i rnam pa dang yon tan dang dbyibs dang /kha dog dang rgyan gyis mthar phyin pa ni bskal pa brgya stong grangs med par 
105b1(phal chen): yang khong du chud bar ma nus te/pad mo chen po de yang de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku skyil krung du bzhugs pas khyab cing yongs su rgyas par mthong ngo//de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku de yang 'di nas bzung 
105b2(phal chen): ba nas srid pa'i rtse mo'i bar gyis mtha' klas par mthong ngo//de bzhi gshegs pa de'i gdan gyi rgyan bsam gyis mi khyab pa yang mthong /'khor gyi dkyil 'khor gyi rgyan bsam gyis mi khyabapa dang /

105b4(phal chen): gi mthu dang /sangs rgyas kyi rnam par 'phrul pa bsam gyis mi khyab pa rang /de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku yi mdog rnam par dag pa bsam gyis mi khyab par dang /spyi gtsug ltar mi mthong ba bsam 
105b5(phal chen): gyis mi khyab pa dang /ljags yangs pa bsam gyis mi khyab ba yang mthong ngo//sangs rgyas kyi gsung gi rgyan bsam gyis mi khyab ba dang /dbyangs kyi dkyil 'khor bsam gyis mi khyab pa thos so//
105b6(phal chen): stobs tshad med pa bsam gyis mi khyab padang mi bsnyengs pa'i rgyan rnam par dag pa bsam kyis mi khyab pa dang /so so yang dag par rig pa'i stobs mngon par bsgrub pa bsam gyis mi khyab payang rjes 
105b7(phal chen): su shes so//sngon byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa yang dag par bsgrub pa bsam gyis ma khyab pa yang rjes su dran no//mngon par rdzogs bar byang chub pa'i rnam par 'phrul pa bsams gyis mi khyab 
105b8(phal chen): pa yang mthong ngo//chos kyi sprin gyi 'brug sgra mngon par bsgrags pa bsam gyis mi khyab pa yang yongs su bstan pa rnam par dmigs pa'i gnas dang /rgyan bsam gyis mi khyab pa'ang thos so//
106a1(phal chen): @#//g.yas g.yon gyi sku'i rnam pa tshad med pa bsam gyis mi khyab pa dang /sems can gyi don dang dgos pa bsam gyi mi khyab pa yongs su grub par 'gyur ba'ang mthong ngo //
106a2(phal chen): de bzhin gshegs pa des kyang phyag g.yas pa brkyang zhing kho bo'i mgo la byug nas/chos kyi rnam grangs kun nas mig ces bya ba de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi yul byang chub sems dpa'i 
106a3(phal chen): spyod pa rab tu brjod pa chos kyi dbyings kyi gzhi dang /rnam pa tha dad pa thams cad rab tu snang ba chos thams cad kyi dkyil 'khor la yang dag par gzhol zhing snang bar byed pa/zhing gi dkyil 'khor

106a5(phal chen): khams thams cad shin tu tshim par byed pa/sems can thams cad kyi thibs po rab tu snang bar byed pa/sems can tham sa cad kyi bsam pa ji lta ba bzhin du rnam par dmigs pa/
106a6(phal chen): sems can thams cad kyi dbang po'i 'khor lo'i rgyud dang /mthu snang ba'i byang ba yang dag par bstan te/kho bos yang kun nas mig gi chos kyi rnam grangs de blangs so//bzung ngo spel to bsams

106a8(phal chen): kyi tshul re re dang chos kyi tshig rnam par phye ba re re las kyang zad pa'am/'grel pa'am/'byam pa'am/mthar thug pa'am/mtha' zad par 'gyur ba dmigs par mi rung ngo rigs kyi bu

106b5(phal chen): nam mkha'i snying po'i gzungs kyi 'od snang bar le'u grangs med pa zhig rab tu phye ba'o//skar ma brtsegs pa'i gzungs kyi 'od sna bas le'u grangs med pa zhig shin tu rgyas par byas so//rgya mtsho

106b7(phal chen): gyi dbang po'am/dri za dang dri za'i dbang po dang /lha ma yin dang lha ma yin gyi dbang po'am/nam mkha' lding dang nam mkha' lding gi dbang po'am/mi'am ci dang mi'am ci'i dbang po'am//
106b8(phal chen): lto 'phye chen po'am lto 'phye chen po'i dbang po'am/mi dang mi'i dbang po'am/tshangs pa dang tshangs pa'i dbang po'am/sems can gang su 'ongs kyang rung ste/de dag thams cad de bzhin 
107a1(phal chen): @#//gshegs pa dang /byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa snang ba'i chos kyi rnam grangs kun nas mig ces bya ba 'di nyid la rab tu bkod cing btsud de/de dag kun kyang kun

107b6(phal chen): sprin la lan brgya stong du mar g.yas phyogs su bskor ba byas nas yang dang yang bltas te/dge slong rgya mtsho'i sprin kyi drung nas song ngo//de nas tshong 
107b7(phal chen): dpon gyi bu nor bzangs dge ba'i bshes gnyen gyi gdam ngag de dang /kun nas mig gi chos kyi rnam grangs de yang rjes su dran par byed cing de bzhin gshegs 
108a1(phal chen): @#//pa'i rnam par 'phrul pa de yang rjes su bsam pa dang /chos kyi tshig dang yi ge'i sprin de dag kyang yongs su bzung ba dang /chos kyi sgo rgya mtsho'i tshul de dag la'ang 'jug par bya ba dang chos 
108a2(phal chen): kyi tshul de dag la'ang rnam par blta ba dang /chos kyi le'u dang klong gi tshul de dag la 'jug par bya ba dang /chos kyi nam mkha' de la yang dag par gzhom par bya ba dang /chos kyid kyi la 'khor de yang rjes su 
108a3(phal chen): sbyang bar bya ba dang /chos rin po che'i gling du yang rjes su sems shing mthar gyis lang ka'i gnas rgya mtsho'i dogs shes bya ba gang na bder phyin nas/dge slong shin tu brtan pa mthong bar dga' zhing shar 
108a4(phal chen): phyogs su bltas so//de bzhin du lho phyogs dang /nub phyogs dang byang phyogs dang /byang shar gyi phyogs mtshams dang /shar lho'i phyogs mtshams dang /lho nub kyi phyogs mtshams dang /nub

108a6(phal chen): bskor cing phan tshun du 'chags pa mthong ngo//nam mkha'i dbyings de yang lha'i dbang po rnams kyi dge slong shin tu brtan la mchod pa'i las su bya ba'i phyir/lha rdzas kyi me tog sil ma'i sprin gyis yog pa 
108a7(phal chen): dang|lha rdzas kyi rol mo'i sprin gyi sgra dang /dar gyi ba dan grangs med pa'i rgyan gyis brgyan par mthong ngo//klu'i dbang po rnams kyi nam mkha' de la a ga ru nag po'i spos mngon par 'thul ba'i sprin shin tu mtho 
108a8(phal chen): ba las 'brug sgra bsam gyis mi khyab pa 'byung bar byas pa mthong ngo//mi'am ci'i dbang po rnams kyis nam mkha' la lha rdzas kyi ngag snyan pa'i rgyud kyi bstod pa'i rol mo brdung ba dang /dkrol ba dang glur 
108b1(phal chen): blang ba'i dbyangs thams cad sbyar cing blangs ba thos par gyur to//lto 'phye chen po'i dbang po rnams kyi nam mkha' de la gos shin tu phra zhing mdog bzang ba'i sprin bsam gyis mi khyab pa de dag yid dga' bas bkye ba de dge slong 
108b2(phal chen): shin tu brtan pa la dad cing mos pa'i gzugs su phyung ba mthong ngo//lha ma yin gyi dbang po rnams kyis kyang nam mkha' de la nor bu rin po che'i sprin bsam gyis mi khyab? pa byin gyis rlabs te/yon tan bsam gyis mi khyab 
108b3(phal chen): pa'i rgyan snang ba dang ldan par snang bar byas so//mthong ngo nam mkha' lding gi dbang po mang po yang mi yi gzugs dang mdog dang dbyibs su 'khod cing nam mkha' lding gi dbang? po'i bu mos kun nas bskor te/rtsod pa med 
108b4(phal chen): pa la dga' zhing thal mo sbyar te gnas bsam gyis mi khyab pa mthong ngo//gnod sbyin gyi dbang po brgya stong 'khor dang bcas pa bsam gyis ma khyab pa dag kyang nam mkha' de la shin tu mi sdug pa'i gzugs su dge slong 
108b5(phal chen): shin tu brtan? pa la byams pa'i mthu dang ldan par 'khod pa mthong ngo//srin po'i dbang po brgya stong 'khod dang bcas pa bsam gyis? mi khyab pa dag kyang nam ma kha' de la dge slong shin tu brtan pa yongs su bsrung ba

120b7(phal chen): bsrel rnam par dgod par snang ngo//sangs rgyas kyi zhing la lan ni lha dang mi rnams de bzhin gshegs pa'i mchod rten la rgyan gyi klubs par byed par snang ste/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das de dag gis sems 
120b8(phal chen): can gyi ris rnam pa tha dad pa dang /sems can gyi 'jig rten gyi rnam pa tha dad pa dang /sems can gyi lam rnam pa tha dad pa dang //sems can gyi skye gnas rnam pa tha dad pa dang sems can gyi tshogs rnam

121a4(phal chen): tshogs rnam pa tha dad pa dang nyon mongs pa dang /bag chags sna tshogs bag la nyal bar gyur pa'i sems can 'byam klas pa rnams la sangs rgyas kyi rnam par 'phrul pa rnam pa sna tshogs yongs su bstan pa dang /

121a6(phal chen): rig pa'i tshul gyi gzhung sna tshogs dang /'phags pa'i bden pa'i ming rgya mtsho'i rgyud rnam pa sna tshogs dang /sangs rgyas kyi khyu mchog dang seng+ge'i nga ro rab tu bsgrag pa dang /sems can gyi dge ba'i rtsa ba sna tshogs
